# H09 evidence / Протокол H09

Review required; no automatic HW VERIFIED promotion.

## Review of the stopped OFF diagnostic

Outcome: **FAIL**, physical outputs OFF confirmed by the operator. No output-ON
commands were sent. All 223 error-queue replies were `0, "No error"`.
Firmware response: `1.04-0.01` (panel previously identified Main 1.04 / Aux 0.01).
There were no jumpers or connected load, DMM or scope leads.

The independent snapshot confirmed V-set 1/1.5/2 V, I-set 0.1/0.2/0.3 A and
enabled Max Volt Set values 2/3/4 V. After Series ON, selecting CH2 returned
INST:NSEL?=1. Registers in this context returned VOLT?=1, CURR?=0.1,
VOLT:LIM?=5 and VOLT:LIM:STAT?=0. The strict independent-channel snapshot rejected
the alias and stopped. Individual settings after leaving Series were not captured;
this run does not establish whether stored individual protections changed.

Cleanup sent global OFF, disabled Series, confirmed OFF through the then-current
driver and returned Local. Physical OFF was independently confirmed by the operator.
The old driver did not validate each selected address before its output-state query;
the new selection check and post-combine protection revalidation are SIM TESTED.
The revised diagnostic records requested versus actual selection without labeling
aliased registers as CH2, and checks individual state again after Independent.
Full offline regression: 106/106 PASS. Hardware replay is required; powered H09
remains NOT_RUN. Raw evidence below is preserved from the failed revision.
